# Goal
# can you please break the project settings down to more tabs

mainly want aliases in their own tab like skills . And maybe a one for custom file/web addresses. generally just neeeds to be easier to use. Maybe worth adding a settings search to the top right thats unused for project settings 

![image.png](assets/image.png)
